Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The projective variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 184 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 18 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the projective decomposition. By minimality, the element a^184 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 18-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 3. QED.
